Empirically, TRACE-CRC achieves reliable trajectory-level coverage with substantially smaller uncertainty balls than conservative multi-step corrections, while avoiding the trajectory undercoverage of compact stepwise and adaptive conformal baselines.

</details>


### [277] [Ultra Low-Power, Lightweight, Probabilistic RSS-Based Path Reconstruction: A System for Landscape-Scale Bee Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27152)
*Christopher J. Noroozi,Joseph L. Woodgate,Michael Mangan,Michael T. Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于RSS的低功耗定位方法，利用旋转高增益发射器和概率建模推断到达角，结合高斯过程和双随机变分推理重构移动路径，实现对38mg超轻量接收器的15米精度追踪（功耗<180uW），并在更多测量下提升至10米精度（功耗<600uW），成功应用于大黄蜂巢穴返回飞行追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动生态学、物联网和机器人学等领域需要定位过小且功率受限、无法实现GNSS的设备。现有基于RSS推断AoA的低功耗定位方法受限于通信范围和大量RSS测量所需的高功耗。

Method: 采用简单的旋转高增益发射器（通信范围300米），仅需极少量RSS测量，通过概率建模推断AoA；利用高斯过程建模接收器运动路径，并采用双随机变分推理进行路径重构。

Result: 在可扩展景观范围内，对重38mg（含电源）的接收器实现约15米定位精度，功耗低于180uW；增加RSS测量次数后，精度提升至约10米，功耗低于600uW。系统成功应用于追踪大黄蜂（Bombus terrestris）的巢穴返回飞行。

Conclusion: 该方法为超轻量、低功耗移动接收器的远距离精准追踪提供了可行方案，显著降低了功耗和测量开销，有望支持飞行昆虫行为学研究等领域的应用。

Abstract: Applications in fields such as movement ecology, Internet of Things or robotics share the need for systems that localize devices that are too small and power constrained to implement GNSS (Global Navigation Satellite Systems). Alternative low-power localization methods often rely on only measurements of RSS (Received Signal Strength) to infer the AoA (Angle of Arrival) of a transmitted radio frequency signal, but are limited by range and the power demand of the large number of RSS measurements required to infer an accurate AoA. In this paper we address these issues with a novel RSS-based method for tracking ultra lightweight and low-power moving receivers across a complex landscape, achieved by using a minimal number of RSS measurements from simple rotating high-gain transmitters with a range of 300m, and applying probabilistic modelling to infer their AoA. The receiver's movement path is then modelled using a Gaussian process and reconstructed using doubly stochastic variational inference, resulting in approximately 15m accuracy tracking of receivers weighing 38mg (including power source) over a scalable landscape range while consuming less than 180uW, increased to approximately 10m accuracy at less than 600uW by taking more RSS measurements. We anticipate that this method will support fields such as the behavioural study of flying insect species, which we demonstrate by applying the system to track Bombus terrestris nest return flights.

</details>


### [278] [Inductive Correlation Clustering with Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27153)
*Francesco Paolo Nerini,Francesco Bonchi,Arijit Khan,André Panisson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图神经网络（GNN）的归纳式相关聚类框架，解决了传统相关聚类算法可扩展性差、仅能归纳式推理的问题，实现了对未见图实例的快速泛化，推理时间降低达5个数量级，近似比在最佳基线10%以内，且在标准基准和图分类池化任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相关聚类（CC）是一种不需预先指定聚类数的组合优化方法，但现有算法存在严重的可扩展性问题，且本质上是归纳式的：每个新问题实例都需从头运行算法。本文旨在利用GNN学习通用的结构模式和节点特征，实现归纳式相关聚类，使模型能泛化到同分布的未见图实例，大幅降低计算开销。

Method: 提出归纳式相关聚类问题，设计基于GNN的框架，在训练阶段学习利用图的结构模式和节点特征，训练后可直接推理新图实例。框架作为可学习的池化机制集成到GNN中，用于图分类任务，捕捉层次化结构信息。

Result: 大量实验表明：在归纳式设置下，推理时间比标准算法降低达5个数量级，近似比维持在最佳基线解的~10%以内；在标准（归纳式）CC基准上也取得有竞争力的结果；作为图分类的可学习池化层，增强了GNN捕捉层次结构信息的能力。

Conclusion: 本文成功将GNN引入相关聚类，首次实现了归纳式相关聚类，显著提升了可扩展性和泛化能力，同时保持了高质量的聚类解。该框架不仅解决了CC的固有局限，还作为高效的可学习池化层推动了图表示学习的发展，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Correlation Clustering (CC) is a natural formulation of clustering in combinatorial optimization, which uses a graph representation of the input and does not require a pre-specified number of clusters. Given $n$ objects and a pairwise similarity function, the goal is to cluster the objects so that similar objects are put in the same cluster and dissimilar objects are put in different clusters. Despite its versatility, existing CC algorithms suffer from significant scalability issues and are inherently transductive: i.e., the algorithm must be executed from scratch for any new problem instance.
  In this work, we bridge this gap by leveraging Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) to solve Inductive Correlation Clustering, a novel generalization of the CC problem designed to handle unseen graph instances. By learning to exploit common structural patterns and node features during training, our framework generalizes to new graphs drawn from the same distribution with minimal computational overhead with respect to standard algorithms. We demonstrate the effectiveness and scalability of our approach through extensive experiments. Our framework not only excels in the inductive setting, e.g., lowering the inference time up to $5$ order of magnitude, while maintaining an approximation ratio within $~10\%$ of the best baseline solution, but also achieves competitive results on standard (transductive) CC benchmarks. Finally, we showcase a practical application of our framework as a learnable pooling mechanism for graph classification. Our results indicate that our method serves as an efficient pooling layer, enhancing the ability of GNNs to capture hierarchical structural information in networks.

</details>


### [279] [Diffusion Policies for Short-Horizon Planning in Robot Crowd Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27158)
*Wendong Li,Jochen Garcke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Planning Diffusion Policy Optimization (PDPO)，一种离线到在线的强化学习框架，利用扩散策略生成短时动作块用于机器人人群导航。首先在避碰演示上预训练，再用 PPO 在线微调，执行时采用五步动作块的滚动时域方式。同时指出基准测试中缺乏边界约束的评估伪影，引入将边界违规视为碰撞的设定，实验显示 PDPO 成功率优于强基线，消融实验证实动作块在修正后的有界基准上尤为关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法在人群导航中通常每步输出单一反应式动作，难以表达多样化的短期避障策略，且常用基准缺乏显式边界约束，导致智能体可离开有效区域绕过密集人群，评估结果失真。

Method: 提出 PDPO 框架：1) 使用扩散策略生成短时动作块（五步）；2) 先在避碰演示数据上离线预训练；3) 将去噪过程视为内部决策过程，采用 PPO 进行在线微调；4) 执行时以滚动时域方式应用动作块。同时引入边界违规即碰撞的评估设定以修正基准伪影。

Result: 在修正后的有界人群导航基准上，PDPO 实现了比强基线更高的成功率；消融实验表明动作块机制对性能提升贡献显著，尤其在有界设定下效果明显。

Conclusion: PDPO 通过扩散策略生成的短时动作块有效捕捉多模态避障行为，离线预训练加在线微调的范式提升了策略在密集动态人群中的鲁棒性；修正基准边界约束对于公平评估至关重要。

Abstract: Robot crowd navigation requires safe and efficient decision-making under dense, dynamic, and multimodal human--robot interactions. Existing reinforcement-learning methods typically output a single reactive action at each timestep, which limits their ability to represent diverse short-term avoidance strategies. We propose Planning Diffusion Policy Optimization (PDPO), an offline-to-online reinforcement-learning framework that uses a diffusion policy to generate short-horizon action chunks for crowd navigation. PDPO is first pretrained on collision-avoidance demonstrations and then fine-tuned online with PPO by treating the denoising process as an internal decision process. During execution, the policy generates a five-step action chunk and applies it in a receding-horizon manner. Furthermore, we observe an evaluation artifact in common crowd-navigation benchmarks: without explicit boundary constraints, learned agents may leave the valid domain and bypass dense crowds. To address this, we introduce a setting in which boundary violations are treated as collisions. Experiments show that PDPO obtains an improved success rate over strong baselines, and ablations demonstrate that action chunks are especially important for the modified bounded benchmark.

</details>


### [280] [TraceBench: Controlled Evaluation of LLM Agents for Time-Series Root-Cause Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27182)
*Tommaso Bendinelli,Artur Dox,Christian Holz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 TraceBench，一个基于仿真的基准框架，用于系统评估 LLM 智能体在时序数据上的根因归因能力。实验发现：领域上下文显著提升性能；智能体倾向通过数值控制台输出而非可视化探索数据；要求生成 Python 脚本映射标签会降低性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体在时序异常检测和根因分析中应用日益广泛，但缺乏在受控条件下的系统性评估，难以量化其真实能力和局限。

Method: 构建 TraceBench 框架，基于三个可解释的机械动力学系统仿真生成受控根因归因任务；每个任务提供时序观测，要求智能体判断系统参数是否被篡改及具体参数；系统评估四种 LLM 智能体在多种受控实验条件下的表现。

Result: 1) 提供领域上下文能大幅提升智能体性能；2) 智能体主要通过数值控制台输出探索数据，而非可视化图表；3) 要求智能体编写 Python 脚本将每个时序样本映射到根因标签，比直接提交预测结果的性能更差。

Conclusion: TraceBench 为 LLM 智能体在动力学系统时序分析任务上的能力提供了可复现、可控的评估基准，揭示了智能体在数据探索方式和任务形式上的关键行为差异，为后续改进智能体设计提供实证指导。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly applied to anomaly detection and root-cause analysis in time-series observations collected from real-world systems; however, their performance on these tasks has not been systematically evaluated under controlled conditions. We introduce TraceBench, a simulation-based framework for generating controlled root-cause attribution tasks. In each generated task, an agent receives time-series observations produced by simulating a physical dynamical system and must determine whether a system parameter was altered during the simulation and, if so, which one. Using TraceBench, we generate tasks from three interpretable mechanical systems and systematically evaluate four LLM agents across controlled experimental conditions, yielding new insights into how these agents analyze time-series observations from dynamical systems. Our results show that agents benefit substantially from domain context and explore data primarily through numerical console output rather than visualizations. We also find that agents generally perform worse when required to produce a Python script that maps each time-series sample to a predicted root-cause label than when they submit predictions directly. We release our datasets, agent trajectories, experimental results, and a leaderboard on our website, tracebench.github.io.

</details>


### [281] [When Interference Graphs Evolve: Doubly Robust Estimation of Dynamic Peer Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27187)
*Xiaojing Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对动态网络中同伴效应的估计问题，提出了受控对比框架与 DynaNet-DR 估计器，解决了预分配网络历史、动态同伴暴露和分配后网络演变三种不同因果角色的识别问题，并给出了双重稳健估计器及其有限样本实现，通过半合成实验验证了估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在交互图随时间演变的情况下，同伴效应估计面临三重挑战：预分配网络历史、动态同伴暴露、分配后网络变化分别扮演不同的因果角色，传统中介分解无法区分这些角色，需要一个能同时索引自身处理、时间聚合同伴暴露和演变摘要的因果框架。

Method: 提出受控对比框架，将潜在结果按自身处理、时间聚合同伴暴露和分配后演变摘要索引，定义自身处理、同伴暴露、受控网络演变和联合受控对比；开发动态网络双重稳健估计器 DynaNet-DR，结合时间分解倾向得分与归一化增广，在一致性、摘要充分性、序贯可交换性、正值性、麻烦函数收敛和弱依赖假设下，当结果回归或倾向得分估计其一致时，典范估计器一致；实现中加入代表性得分预测、固定截尾和有限样本稳定化。

Result: 在固定真实时序图序列上的半合成基准测试显示，DynaNet-DR 在针对完整效应谱的方法中估计精度表现优异；基准测试评估的是摘要索引对比而非反事实边生成；MathOverflow 观测性研究作为假设下的说明性应用。

Conclusion: 论文建立了动态网络中同伴效应的因果识别框架与双重稳健估计方法，有效区分了网络演变不同阶段的因果角色，并提供了实用的估计器实现与实验验证，为时序网络干预效应分析提供了新工具。

Abstract: Peer effects are difficult to estimate when interaction graphs evolve because pre-assignment network history, dynamic peer exposure, and post-assignment network change have distinct causal roles. We introduce a controlled contrast framework that indexes potential outcomes by own treatment, temporally aggregated peer exposure, and a post-assignment evolution summary. Differences between the resulting means define own-treatment, peer-exposure, controlled network-evolution, and joint controlled contrasts rather than a mediation decomposition. We develop the Dynamic Network Doubly Robust estimator, DynaNet-DR, which combines a temporally factorized propensity with normalized augmentation. Under consistency, summary sufficiency, sequential exchangeability, positivity, nuisance convergence, and weak dependence, its canonical estimator is consistent when either the outcome regression or the propensity estimator is consistent. The reported implementation adds representative-score prediction, fixed clipping, and finite-sample stabilization. Semi-synthetic benchmarks on fixed real temporal graph sequences show favorable estimation accuracy among methods targeting the full profile. These benchmarks assess summary-indexed contrasts rather than counterfactual edge generation, and the MathOverflow study is an observational illustration under the stated assumptions.

</details>


### [282] [Common Geodesics Do Not Guarantee Fisher Consistency of the Structured SVM: Minimal Counterexamples and a Tree-Metric Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27203)
*Jintao Fei,Jiangying Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明了结构化支持向量机 Fisher 一致性的已知必要条件（任务损失为度量且每个输出三元组有公共测地点）对于标准坐标逐个 argmax 解码器并不充分。作者完全分类了正权树度量：argmax 一致性成立当且仅当树是一条路径；分支树的失效仅限于边界分布。在满足公共测地条件的度量中，5 个输出是全支撑反例的必要充分条件，K_{2,3} 是无限 K_{m,n} 族中最小的成员。还给出了三维汉明立方的全支撑反例。所有最优性声明都有精确的原始-对偶证书。反例揭示了具体的解码器差距：嵌入可保证校准链接的存在，但不能验证每个替代风险最小化器上的指定 argmax 链接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究结构化支持向量机中 Fisher 一致性的充要条件，特别是针对标准坐标逐个 argmax 解码器，揭示已知必要条件（公共测地点条件）的不充分性，并完全刻画树度量下的 argmax 一致性，同时构造最小反例以理解解码器差距。

Method: 理论分析与构造性反例相结合。通过构造具体的分布和分数向量，利用原始-对偶最优性证书验证反例的最优性；对正权树度量进行完全分类，证明路径树当且仅当满足 argmax 一致性；分析边界分布与全支撑分布的差异；在满足公共测地条件的度量族中寻找最小全支撑反例（K_{2,3} 及 K_{m,n} 族、三维汉明立方）。

Result: 1) 四输出单位星形图存在严格非 Bayes 的最优分数向量，四输出是满足公共测地条件下的最小反例。2) 正权树度量中，argmax 一致性当且仅当树是路径；分支树失效仅限于边界分布，全支撑分布下保持 argmax 性质。3) 满足公共测地条件的度量中，五输出是全支撑反例的必要充分条件，K_{2,3} 是无限 K_{m,n} 族最小成员。4) 给出三维汉明立方的全支撑反例。5) 所有最优性声明有精确原始-对偶证书。6) 揭示解码器差距：嵌入可保证校准链接存在，但不能验证每个替代风险最小化器上的指定 argmax 链接。

Conclusion: 论文确立了结构化 SVM 中 argmax 解码器 Fisher 一致性的精确条件，表明公共测地点条件不充分，树度量下一致性完全由拓扑决定（路径树），并通过最小反例揭示了多面体设置中嵌入方法的局限性：校准链接的存在不等于指定 argmax 链接的普遍有效性，这对结构化预测损失函数设计有重要启示。

Abstract: A known necessary condition for Fisher consistency of the structured support vector machine requires the task loss to be a metric for which every output triple has a common geodesic point. We show that this condition is not sufficient for the canonical coordinate-wise argmax decoder. A four-output unit star admits an exactly optimal score vector whose maximizers are all strictly non-Bayes, and four outputs are minimal among metrics satisfying the condition. We then completely classify positively weighted tree metrics whose vertex set is the output space: argmax consistency holds if and only if the tree is a path. The failure on branching trees is confined to boundary distributions; every tree retains the argmax property at every full-support distribution. Among metrics satisfying the common-geodesic condition, five outputs are necessary and sufficient for a full-support counterexample; $K_{2,3}$ is the smallest member of an infinite $K_{m,n}$ family. We additionally give a full-support counterexample for the three-dimensional Hamming cube. All optimality claims have exact primal-dual certificates. The counterexamples expose a concrete decoder gap: in this polyhedral setting, an embedding can guarantee the existence of a calibrated link without validating a prescribed argmax link on every surrogate-risk minimizer.

</details>


### [283] [Profit based evaluation of machine learning for nitrogen recommendations in winter wheat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27205)
*Xulong Wang,Po Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究评估机器学习在冬小麦氮肥推荐中的表现，发现直接用机器学习预测最优氮肥率不如标准建议盈利，但将机器学习作为标准建议的修正步骤可显著减少利润损失，且无需重新训练即可跨站点泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准英国氮肥建议不随价格调整，而价格波动导致最优氮肥率大幅变化；机器学习常被提议作为解决方案，但通常仅按预测精度评估，准确预测不一定带来更高利润。

Method: 基于两个长期英国实验的892条产量响应曲线构建测试平台，扫描氮肥-粮价比以覆盖所有价格情景，直接以利润损失评分氮肥建议；对比多种机器学习模型、标准建议及修正策略。

Result: 机器学习模型均无法在农场容差内恢复最优氮肥率，且在正常价格下均输给标准建议；简单修正步骤使利润损失减少25%，更好模型和额外特征无增益；同一固定修正在第二站点无需重训练即减少43%损失；标准建议加阻尼修正的混合策略消除偏差并削减极端损失；价格扫描还可为减排定价，成本与当前碳价相当。

Conclusion: 机器学习的价值在于作为标准建议的利润导向修正，而非替代标准建议；直接优化利润指标优于追求预测精度。

Abstract: Nitrogen rates for winter wheat are set before the season, under unknown prices and weather. The standard UK advice does not respond to prices, yet recent price swings moved the most profitable rate by tens of kilograms per hectare. Machine learning is often proposed as the fix. However, it is usually judged on prediction accuracy, and accurate prediction does not by itself make the recommended rate more profitable. Our insight is to score nitrogen advice directly by the profit it forgoes on measured yield response curves. We build a test bench on 892 such curves from two long running UK experiments, and sweep the nitrogen to grain price ratio to cover all price scenarios. On this bench, machine learning fails as a predictor. No model recovers the best rate within farm tolerance, and the benchmark noise shows none can. At normal prices, every model also loses to the standard advice on profit. The gain sits elsewhere. A simple correction step applied after the model cuts profit losses by a quarter, while better models and extra features give no gain. The same frozen correction cuts losses by 43% at the second site without any retraining. A hybrid of standard advice plus a damped correction removes bias and trims rare large losses. The same price sweep also prices emission cuts, at a cost comparable to current carbon prices. Machine learning therefore pays as a profit scored correction to standard advice, not as its replacement.

</details>


### [284] [HALO: A Heterogeneity-Aware Language-Aligned IMU Foundation Model for Open-Set Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27233)
*Zihan Ding,Liyu Zhang,Xiaomin Ouyang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HALO，一种针对IMU的人体活动识别（HAR）基础模型，通过两阶段训练解决传感异构性与开放集泛化难题，在仅35M参数下超越现有SOTA基线，显著提升零样本开放集准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HAR研究缺乏能跨主体、设备、活动泛化的统一模型，主要受限于两大挑战：感知异构性（采样率、通道配置、传感器位置差异）及对未见活动和标签词表的泛化能力差。

Method: 采用两阶段训练框架：第一阶段利用异构感知自监督学习预训练IMU编码器，包含自适应池化分词、通道独立特征提取、注入自然语言传感器描述的上下文传感器条件化；第二阶段通过同义词感知软对比学习将IMU编码器与文本嵌入对齐，支持基于余弦相似度检索的开放集识别，无需针对各数据集训练分类器。

Result: 在10个公开HAR数据集训练、7个保留数据集评测中，HALO在所有8个聚合指标上均优于5个SOTA基线，且在基线匹配输入下的4个设置中有3个保持领先。模型仅约35M可训练参数（比MOMENT的341.2M少10倍），零样本开放集准确率在全部87个训练标签上提升13.7个百分点。但在两个分布严重偏移的数据集上，包括HALO在内的所有模型零样本均失效。

Conclusion: HALO证明了在极小参数量下，通过异构感知自监督预训练与语言对齐对比学习，可构建跨域泛化的IMU基础模型，显著推进HAR开放集识别；但极端分布偏移仍是零样本泛化的瓶颈，需后续研究攻克。

Abstract: Human Activity Recognition (HAR) using inertial measurement units (IMUs) enables a wide range of applications, yet the field still lacks a unified model that can generalize across diverse subjects, devices, and activities. Training such a model is difficult due to two key challenges: sensing heterogeneity -- differences in sampling rates, channel configurations, and sensor placements -- and poor generalization to unseen activities and label vocabularies. We introduce HALO (Heterogeneity-Aware Language-aligned Open-set model), a domain-specific IMU foundation model that addresses both challenges through a two-stage training framework. Stage 1 pretrains the IMU encoder with heterogeneity-aware self-supervised learning, including adaptive-pooling tokenization, channel-independent feature extraction, and contextualized sensor conditioning that injects natural-language sensor descriptions into each channel embedding. Stage 2 aligns this IMU encoder with text embeddings via synonym-aware soft contrastive learning, enabling open-set recognition via cosine-similarity retrieval without per-dataset classifiers. Trained on 10 public HAR datasets and evaluated on 7 held-out datasets, HALO outperforms five state-of-the-art baselines on all 8 aggregate metrics, and still leads on 3 of 4 settings under baseline-matched inputs. Despite using only ~35M trainable parameters -- 10x fewer than the latest foundation model MOMENT (341.2M) -- HALO improves zero-shot open-set accuracy, measured over all 87 training labels, by 13.7 percentage points. On two further datasets with severe distribution shift, every model including HALO collapses zero-shot. A video demonstration of HALO's performance in real world is available at https://youtu.be/rooVKragtFU

</details>


### [285] [Importance Scoring of Transformer Attention Heads in Learning Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27241)
*Ahmad Jad Allah,Kazi F. Akhter,Md. Kamrozzaman Bhuiyan,Manar D. Samad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种重要性评分指标，用于解释表格数据上的多头Transformer模型，实验表明基于该指标剔除低重要性注意力头可保持模型性能，而移除高重要性头会导致性能大幅下降，且重要头分布在各层无固定模式，不同表格数据集的头重要性差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度Transformer在计算机视觉和自然语言处理中已被广泛研究，但在表格数据上的应用相对较少；现有模型计算量大且不透明，需要通过分析数据变换来理解和优化模型，因此需要一种针对表格数据的注意力头重要性评估方法。

Method: 提出一种重要性评分指标，用于量化多头Transformer中各注意力头的重要性；在40个多样化的表格数据集上进行实验，逐步移除不同重要性得分的头，观察模型性能变化，并分析六层注意力网络中重要头的分布规律。

Result: 在72.5%的实验中，移除最低重要性头时模型性能下降最小；移除最高重要性头导致分类性能下降最大；重要头分散在各层，无层级一致性趋势；不同表格数据集的头重要性差异远大于图像和语言领域。

Conclusion: 提出的重要性评分能有效识别冗余注意力头，提升Transformer在表格数据上的效率；该指标为解释和优化表格数据Transformer提供了新工具，源代码已公开。

Abstract: Computationally demanding and opaque deep learning models can be better understood and optimized by analyzing how they transform data. While deep transformers have been widely studied in computer vision and natural language processing, their application in tabular data remains relatively underexplored. This paper presents one of the first applications of an importance-scoring metric to interpret multi-head transformer models in learning from tabular data. Experiments conducted on 40 diverse tabular datasets demonstrate robustness to head drops based on the proposed head importance score. In 72.5\% of experimental examples, the model remains most resilient to performance drops when heads with the lowest importance scores are gradually removed. In contrast, removing the most important attention head first results in the greatest reduction in classification performance. A closer look at individual head importance scores across six attention layers reveals that important heads are scattered across layers, with no consistent layer-specific trends. In contrast to the image and language domains, the importance of individual attention heads varies considerably across tabular datasets with different schemas and feature spaces. The proposed importance score can improve efficiency and redundancy within transformer architectures. We make the source code for measuring the importance of individual attention heads publicly available.

</details>


### [286] [Circuit Condensation: Post-Training that Concentrates a Behavior's Causal Circuit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27254)
*Sai Adith Senthil Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出「电路压缩」方法，通过后训练将模型行为压缩进更小的因果图，显著减少可解释电路的规模并保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机制可解释性方法（冻结发现）返回数百条边，难以全面检查、比对或验证；需要更紧凑、可验证的因果电路。

Method: 电路压缩：迭代剪除低归因边，并训练低秩适配器匹配原模型在剩余边上的行为，仅在任务性能与通用能力均保持时保留剪切。

Result: 在 4 个行为、8 个模型上，压缩电路在 30/32 设置下更小，平均缩小 8.1×、最大 316×；权重更新而非搜索本身驱动压缩；19 电路中 11 个不可再压缩，其余含可移除边；成对消融揭示边间依赖；间接宾语识别任务中仅 24 头（17 个有文档记录），远少于冻结基线的 61 头（36 个无记录），且能追踪原模型下一词分布并预测错误。

Conclusion: 电路压缩能生成已发表机制的充分子电路而非重构，兼具紧凑性、可验证性与预测原模型行为的能力。

Abstract: One approach to mechanistic interpretability explains behavior through circuits: the components and connections that carry it. Frozen discovery often returns hundreds of edges, making them hard to inspect, compare, or verify exhaustively. We introduce Circuit Condensation, which post-trains models to concentrate behaviors into smaller causal graphs. Each round prunes low-attribution edges and trains a low-rank adapter to match the original through what remains, retaining the cut only if task performance and general capability survive. Across four behaviors and eight models, condensed circuits are smaller than the strongest frozen baseline in 30 of 32 settings, by $8.1\times$ on average and up to $316\times$. Repeating the search without weight updates produces larger circuits in 29 of 32 settings, showing that weight updates, rather than search alone, drive the reduction. Testing every subset of 19 circuits finds 11 that cannot be reduced and reveals removable edges in the rest. Pair ablations expose dependencies between edges, showing that their effects cannot be understood independently. On indirect object identification, condensation isolates 24 heads, 17 of them with documented roles, against 61 heads and 36 undocumented ones for the matched frozen circuit: a sufficient sub-circuit of the published mechanism rather than a reconstruction of it. The resulting circuit tracks the original model's next-token distribution and predicts its errors.

</details>


### [287] [Making Latent Evolution Explicit: Operator-Structured Transitions for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27259)
*Xiaoxiao Lu,Yunlong Dong,Jiahao Shi,Ye Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Latent Evolution Operator Network (LEON)，一种基于受控Koopman算子视角的潜在空间演化建模方法，通过上下文调制的算子传播和加性强迫项来实现状态转移，优于基于Transformer的token交互式预测器，在两种WAM公式化中显著提升闭环性能和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有World Action Models (WAMs)虽在潜在空间预测任务相关场景演化，但普遍采用以token交互为中心的Transformer预测器，缺乏面向时序演化的归纳偏置。文中将转移实现作为独立于预测表示和预测-策略耦合的架构选择进行研究，旨在引入符合动力学系统演化特性的架构先验。

Method: LEON在学习的可观测空间中建模潜在演化：采用上下文调制的算子传播捕捉共享的演化算子结构，辅以加性强迫项处理互补变化。该设计基于受控Koopman生成器视角，将上下文依赖的转移变化组织在共享算子结构周围，保留加性变化路径。

Result: 在受控动力学系统上验证了演化专用归纳偏置的有效性及算子传播与强迫项的互补作用。在两种不同集成潜在预测策略的WAM公式化中，LEON均提升闭环性能和鲁棒性，且在完全替换转移模块时仍保持有效性。

Conclusion: 转移实现是潜在WAMs中至关重要的架构选择。LEON通过引入基于Koopman算子的演化建模范式，为潜在空间状态转移提供了更符合物理演化规律的架构先验，显著提升了机器人策略的闭环表现。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) augment robot policies by predicting how task-relevant scene states may evolve under interaction. Recent WAMs increasingly perform such prediction in latent representation spaces, avoiding full appearance-level generation while preserving control-relevant information. Yet latent transitions are commonly realized with Transformer-based predictors whose inductive structure is centered on token interaction rather than temporal evolution. We study transition realization as an architectural choice distinct from predictive representation and prediction-policy coupling. We introduce the Latent Evolution Operator Network (LEON), which models latent evolution in a learned observable space through context-modulated operator-based propagation and additive forcing. Grounded in the controlled Koopman generator view of evolution, LEON organizes context-dependent transition variation around a shared evolution-operator structure while retaining a complementary path for additive change. Controlled dynamical systems verify the resulting evolution-specific inductive bias and the complementary roles of operator propagation and forcing. Across two WAM formulations that integrate latent prediction into the policy differently, LEON improves closed-loop performance and robustness while remaining effective under full transition replacement. These results establish transition realization as a consequential architectural choice in latent WAMs.

</details>


### [288] [MM-Spectrum: Multimodal Multi-spectral Molecular Structural Elucidation with a Stable MoE Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27286)
*Hai-tao Yu,Nan Min,Zheng Fang,Hongyu Zhan,Yusen Tan,Yuhan Wang,Jun Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MM-Spectrum，一种稀疏专家混合框架，用于从多模态光谱数据推断分子结构，通过模态感知路由、共享与交互专家及异构专家容量解决多模态不平衡问题，在全模态、双模态及缺失模态设定下均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接拼接多光谱序列存在异常性能下降，主要源于模态间显著的异质性导致的多模态不平衡，现有方法难以有效融合互补且高度异构的光谱信号。

Method: MM-Spectrum引入显式模态感知路由机制，将光谱身份暴露给路由器；设置共享专家与交互专家，配合异构专家容量，提取模态独特信息与跨模态协同信息，同时抑制噪声干扰。

Result: 在分子结构阐释任务的全模态、双模态及缺失模态三种设定下，MM-Spectrum均实现持续且显著的性能提升，消融实验与可解释性分析提供支持。

Conclusion: MM-Spectrum通过针对多光谱不平衡设计的稀疏MoE架构，有效整合异构光谱信号，为多模态分子结构推断提供了鲁棒且可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Inferring molecular structures from multimodal spectroscopic measurements requires integrating complementary yet highly heterogeneous signals. However, the common paradigm of directly concatenating multispectral sequences can exhibit anomalous performance degradation, primarily due to pronounced heterogeneity and the resulting multimodal imbalance across modalities. As a remedy, we propose MM-Spectrum, a sparse Mixture-of-Experts framework tailored for multimodal multispectral spectra-to-structure elucidation. To better match the information characteristics under multispectral imbalance, MM-Spectrum introduces an explicit modality-aware routing mechanism that exposes spectral identity to the router in addition to token content representations. Moreover, it incorporates shared and interaction experts, together with heterogeneous expert capacities, to extract multispectral modality-unique and cross-modal synergistic information while suppressing noise-induced interference. Across full-modality, bimodal, and missing-modality settings on molecular structural elucidation, MM-Spectrum achieves consistent and substantial improvements, supported by ablation studies and interpretability analyses.

</details>


### [289] [QuantumBoostNet: A Hybrid Classical-Quantum Architecture for Enhanced Accuracy in Cardiac Ultrasound View Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27302)
*Mihai Udrescu-Milosav,Stefan-Alexandru Jura,Mihai Udrescu,Gerhard-Paul Diller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QuantumBoostNet是一种混合经典-量子架构，用于心脏超声视图识别，结合经典骨干网络与经典和量子双头，通过两阶段训练和自适应混合参数，在有限量子比特模拟下优于现有模型，且在图像分类基准上表现优异，具有抗噪性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机视觉模型在标准基准上表现良好，但在专业医学成像任务中因高噪声数据而效果不佳，需要更鲁棒的模型。

Method: 提出QuantumBoostNet混合经典-量子架构，包含经典骨干网络、经典头和量子头（10量子比特参数化量子电路），采用两阶段训练，通过监控损失动态的混合参数实现头之间的自适应过渡。

Result: 在心脏超声视图识别任务上持续优于最先进的经典和混合经典-量子模型，在公认图像分类基准上也表现优越，并展现出对噪声的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持继续开发混合经典-量子模型用于专业医学成像应用。

Abstract: Accurate identification of the correct view or angle in cardiac ultrasound (echocardiogram) is a critical component of cardiologic imaging. This step is essential for precise anatomical interpretation, reliable measurement, and the reduction of clinical errors. Although computer vision has advanced significantly, most state-of-the-art models perform well on standard benchmarks but often yield suboptimal results in specialized medical imaging tasks due to the high level of noise present in the data. QuantumBoostNet, a hybrid classical-quantum architecture, is introduced to address these challenges. This model integrates a classical backbone with two heads: one classical and one quantum, with the quantum head implemented as a parametrized 10-qubit quantum circuit. Training occurs in two stages, with an adaptive transition between heads governed by a mixing parameter that monitors loss dynamics. Extensive experiments indicate that, despite the limited number of qubits that can be simulated, QuantumBoostNet consistently outperforms state-of-the-art classical and hybrid classical-quantum models in cardiac ultrasound view identification, achieving a relative improvement over the best competitor. QuantumBoostNet also demonstrates superior performance on established image classification benchmarks and exhibits robustness to noise. These findings support the continued development of hybrid classical-quantum models for specialized medical imaging applications.

</details>


### [290] [Beyond Parallel Blindness: Information Floors and Model Gaps in Block Drafting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27339)
*Xinwei Qiang,Xiang Fang,Chang Chen,Yue Guan,Yufei Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出信息地板概念，将Block drafters的拒绝率分解为不可避免的信息地板（因缺乏块内路径信息）和模型差距（因建模不完美），通过大规模实验发现：全并行地板在Qwen3-4B最后槽位达0.286，单个已实现token可消除86-100%地板，现有drafters仍远高于地板，模型差距占DFlash拒绝率43-64%、DSpark拒绝率85-92%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Block drafters在一次前向传递中提出多个token，其拒绝率混合了两种损失：缺乏块内路径信息导致的不可避免损失，和对可观测信息建模不完美导致的损失。现有的接受长度指标无法区分这两者，需要将其分离以评估短程条件化的价值与提议质量。

Method: 引入信息地板概念——在指定条件阶数下的最小期望拒绝率；拒绝率超过此地板的部分定义为模型差距。通过目标模型回溯在四个领域、四个开放权重目标模型和一个前沿API目标模型上估算两者。

Result: 三个主要发现：1) 全并行地板在Qwen3-4B最后槽位达到0.286，即使最优提议也限制在71%逐槽接受率；2) 一个已实现token可移除86-100%的地板，这种局部性也被独立互信息分析验证；3) 当前drafters仍远高于地板：最后槽位模型差距占DFlash拒绝率43-64%，占DSpark oracle条件拒绝率85-92%。

Conclusion: 研究成功将短程条件化的价值与提议质量分离。信息地板量化了并行解码的理论极限，模型差距揭示了当前drafters的改进空间。单token条件化即可消除大部分地板，说明局部依赖是关键；现有方法主要瓶颈在于建模质量而非并行机制本身。

Abstract: Block drafters propose several tokens in one forward pass, before earlier target tokens are realised. Their rejection mixes two losses: missing within-block path information and imperfect modelling of observable information. Accepted length cannot distinguish them. We separate the two with an information floor, the minimum expected rejection at a specified conditioning order; rejection above this floor is the model gap. Estimating both from target rollouts across four domains, four open-weight targets, and a frontier API target yields three findings. First, the all-parallel floor reaches $0.286$ at the final slot on Qwen3-4B, limiting even the best proposal to $71\%$ per-slot acceptance. Second, one realised token removes $86$--$100\%$ of this floor, a locality also recovered by an independent mutual-information analysis. Third, current drafters remain far above their floors: the final-slot model gap accounts for $43$--$64\%$ of DFlash rejection and $85$--$92\%$ of DSpark's oracle-conditioned rejection. These findings separate the value of short-range conditioning from proposal quality.

</details>


### [291] [Understanding Evolution Strategies for LLM Reasoning: Broader Reasoning Coverage than GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27351)
*Yunpeng Ba,Zhi Zheng,Yue Xie,Jiaqing Li,Xialiang Tong,Tao Zhong,Mingxuan Yuan,Zhichao Lu,Xuyang Wu,Zhenkun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了进化策略(ES)在LLM推理后训练中的优化行为，发现ES相比GRPO具有更广的推理覆盖度和更高的Pass@K性能，且参数更新呈现功能性稀疏性，不导致灾难性遗忘；提出GRPO-ES顺序训练策略，证明ES是独特的推理后训练范式而非GRPO的劣质替代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ES作为一种内存高效的LLM推理后训练范式，其优化行为研究不足，难以界定其相对于主流范式(如GRPO)的优势边界，需要系统分析ES的动态机制以明确其定位。

Method: 通过理论分析(验证器投影的Jensen-Shannon多样性与Pass@K关系)和实证研究(对比ES与GRPO的熵崩塌、Pass@1/Pass@K表现、参数漂移稀疏性、超参数敏感性)，并设计GRPO-ES顺序训练策略。

Result: 1) ES理论和实证上优于GRPO，实现更广推理覆盖和更高Pass@K，同时提升Pass@1；2) ES参数更新呈功能性稀疏性，大幅参数漂移不导致广泛功能改变或灾难性遗忘；3) ES在更大LLM中需更小种群规模；4) GRPO-ES顺序策略结合两者优势。

Conclusion: ES是独特的推理后训练范式，具有独特优势(更广覆盖、功能稀疏性、无灾难遗忘)，而非仅作为GRPO的内存高效但效果较差的替代方案。

Abstract: Evolution Strategies (ES) have recently emerged as a memory-efficient post-training paradigm for LLM reasoning. However, the optimization behavior of ES remains understudied, making it hard to define its advantage scope compared to mainstream post-training paradigms (e.g., Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)). By systematically investigating ES dynamics and mechanisms, this paper first identifies a performance advantage of ES over GRPO, theoretically and empirically showing that ES can lead to broader reasoning coverage, thereby better exploiting the reasoning capabilities of pretrained LLMs. Theoretically, we show that verifier-projected Jensen-Shannon diversity across the ES population is helpful to higher Pass@K performances. Empirically, unlike GRPO, which exhibits entropy collapse, ES improves Pass@1 while attaining higher Pass@K than GRPO. We further develop a sequential GRPO-ES training strategy that combines GRPO's strength in Pass@1 with ES's gains in Pass@K. Second, we find that despite substantial whole-model parameter drift, the task-performance gains of ES are only contributed to a sparse subset of larger-magnitude updates. This functional sparsity suggests that large parameter movement need not imply widespread functional change, and held-out evaluations further show that it does not necessarily lead to catastrophic forgetting. Finally, we study how hyperparameter design affects the effectiveness of ES, demonstrating that ES requires a smaller population size in a larger LLM. These findings position ES as a distinct reasoning post-training paradigm rather than a less effective, memory-efficient alternative to GRPO.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [292] [Assessing the Downstream Utility of Evidence-Aware Retrieval in RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26379)
*Utshab Kumar Ghosh,Debayan Mukhopadhyay,Shubham Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了在检索增强生成（RAG）中，基于答案支持证据的检索评估信号是否能提升下游决策的可靠性。实验发现，该信号虽改变检索排序，但其下游价值并不一致：不能可靠改进检索器训练，系统选择的收益取决于生成器指令，基于该信号的检索分数难以稳健预测未见主题的回答质量，且人工过滤虽保留有用证据，但不同评估者对回答质量提升的结论不一。结论指出，让检索评估更贴近生成所需证据，并不自动使所有下游用例更可靠，RAG 评估方法应针对具体比较、决策和结论进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 RAG 系统的发展，检索评估逐渐从主题相关性转向关注检索段落是否包含支持生成的证据。本文旨在研究这种与下游证据需求更紧密对齐的评估方式，是否真的能让基于检索评估的各类决策（如检索器比较、训练指导、系统选择、回答质量预测、证据过滤）更加可靠。

Method: 在五个检索基准和一个端到端的 TREC RAG 2025 设置上，考察答案支持信号在四种角色中的表现：比较检索器、指导检索训练与系统选择、预测下游回答质量、过滤提供给生成器的证据。同时进行人工标注干预实验，验证过滤后证据的保留效果及不同评估者对回答质量的判断。

Result: 答案支持信号会改变检索排序，但其下游效用不一：1) 不能可靠提升检索器训练效果；2) 用于系统选择时，收益取决于生成器如何被指令使用证据；3) 基于该信号的检索分数无法稳健预测未见主题的回答质量；4) 人工过滤实验证实过滤倾向于保留包含有用答案证据的段落，但不同回答评估者对最终回答是否改进得出相反结论。

Conclusion: 使检索评估更贴近生成所需证据，本身并不保证所有基于该评估的下游用例都更可靠。因此，RAG 评估方法应根据其预期支持的具体比较、决策和结论进行针对性评估，而非假设统一评估指标适用于所有场景。

Abstract: Retrieval evaluation for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is increasingly designed around whether retrieved passages contain evidence that can support generation, rather than topical relevance alone. We study whether this closer alignment with downstream evidence needs also makes retrieval evaluation more useful for the decisions built from it.
  Across five retrieval benchmarks and an end-to-end TREC RAG 2025 setting, we examine an answer-support signal in four roles: comparing retrievers, guiding retrieval training and system selection, predicting downstream answer quality, and filtering the evidence supplied to a generator. The signal changes retrieval rankings, but its downstream value is not uniform. It does not reliably improve retriever training; the benefit of using it for system selection depends on how the generator is instructed to use the retrieved evidence; and retrieval scores based on it do not robustly predict answer quality on unseen topics. In a direct evidence intervention, human annotators confirm that filtering preferentially preserves passages containing useful answer evidence, yet different answer evaluators reach different conclusions about whether the resulting answers improve.
  These results show that making retrieval evaluation more closely reflect the evidence needed for generation does not by itself make every downstream use of that evaluation more reliable. RAG evaluation methods should therefore be assessed with respect to the particular comparisons, decisions, and conclusions they are intended to support.

</details>


### [293] [Preference Flow Matching with Spectral Factorization for Micro-video Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26579)
*Xinxin Dong,Haokai Ma,Fei Hu,YuZe Zheng,Bin Wu,Yonghui Yang,Xiaodong Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 PrismRec，一种基于频谱分解的偏好流匹配框架，用于微视频推荐。通过频谱语义分解（SSF）将帧级表示解耦为静态语义与动态因子，再经上下文校准偏好匹配（CPM）注入用户个性化敏感度，使视频内容成为偏好形成的内在驱动力。实验在四个数据集上超越 SOTA 最高 22.65%，且推理开销与显存占用最低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微视频推荐方法将帧序列压缩为单一整体表示，导致稳定的视觉语义与演化的动态特征纠缠不清；同时基于扩散/流匹配的推荐器仅以粗粒度行为上下文为条件，忽略了上下文内部的时间结构，限制了偏好建模的精度与效率。

Method: 1. 频谱语义分解（SSF）：在时域频谱域引入先验引导的可学习频率掩码，将帧级表示分解为互补的静态语义因子与动态因子。2. 上下文校准偏好匹配（CPM）：根据每个用户对静态/动态因子的敏感度加权融合，并将校准后的上下文作为结构化条件注入流匹配过程，引导轨迹逼近目标视频表示。

Result: 在来自两个平台的四个公开数据集上，PrismRec 相比最强基线最高提升 22.65%，且在所有对比方法中推理延迟最低、峰值显存占用最小。

Conclusion: PrismRec 通过频谱层面的语义-动态解耦与个性化上下文校准，将视频内容从辅助侧信息提升为偏好形成的核心驱动力，实现了高精度、低开销的微视频推荐。

Abstract: Micro-video recommendation aims to infer user preferences from historical interactions and multimodal video content, thereby identifying the next video of interest. However, prevailing methods compress frame sequences into a single holistic representation, entangling the stable visual semantics and the evolving dynamics that jointly shape user preferences. Meanwhile, diffusion- and flow matching-based recommenders condition their generation process solely on coarse behavioral context, leaving its internal temporal structure outside preference formation. We therefore propose PrismRec, a Preference Flow Matching framework with Spectral Factorization for Micro-video Recommendation. Analogous to a prism that disperses white light into its constituent spectrum, PrismRec devises Spectral Semantic Factorization (SSF) to derive complementary static semantic and dynamic factors from frame-level representations via a prior-guided learnable frequency mask in the temporal frequency domain. Then, it proposes Context-Calibrated Preference Matching (CPM) to weigh them with each user's specific sensitivity and inject the calibrated context as a structured condition to steer the matching trajectory toward the target representation, making video content as an intrinsic driver of preference formation rather than auxiliary side information. Experiments on four datasets from two platforms show that PrismRec surpasses the SOTA baseline by up to 22.65%, with the lowest inference cost and peak memory among the compared methods.

</details>


### [294] [hoBIT: A Profile-Aware Retrieval-Augmented Chatbot for University Academic Advising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26604)
*Yoonseo Kim,Seongmin Lee,Joongheon Kim,SeongKu Kang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对大学学业咨询中相同问题需根据学生院系、入学年份、学位项目等档案差异给出不同答案的问题，提出 proFILL 方法，将基于规则的咨询聊天机器人 hoBIT 转化为档案感知的检索增强生成（RAG）系统，无需预先获取完整用户档案，而是根据查询意图和初步检索证据渐进式获取所需档案属性，并在档案感知索引上进行条件检索。实验表明 proFILL 优于多种 RAG 基线，受目标用户偏好，且在开放权重模型上仍保持有效，适合低成本本地部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于档案的检索器忽略用户档案差异（如院系、入学批次、学位项目），导致相同问题检索到看似合理但不适用的证据，无法满足个性化学业咨询需求。

Method: proFILL 方法：不要求预先提供完整用户档案，而是根据查询意图和初步检索到的证据，渐进式获取当前查询所需的档案属性，并利用这些属性在档案感知索引上进行条件检索，将规则制聊天机器人 hoBIT 转化为档案感知 RAG 系统。

Result: 大量实验和人类偏好研究表明，proFILL 在多个 RAG 基线上表现更优，受目标用户青睐，且在开放权重模型上依然有效，支持低成本本地部署。

Conclusion: proFILL 通过渐进式档案获取和档案感知检索，有效解决了学业咨询中个性化答案生成的问题，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: In university academic advising, identical questions can require different answers depending on a student's department, admission cohort, and degree program, causing profile-blind retrievers to surface plausible but inapplicable evidence. We present proFILL, a method for transforming hoBIT, our college's current rule-based advising chatbot, into a profile-aware retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) system. Rather than requiring a complete user profile upfront, proFILL progressively acquires only the profile attributes needed for each query, guided by both the query intent and the initially retrieved evidence, and uses them to condition retrieval over a profile-aware index. Extensive experiments and a human preference study show that proFILL outperforms diverse RAG baselines, is preferred by target users, and remains effective with open-weight models for cost-effective on-premise deployment.

</details>


### [295] [When Does Supervised Fine-Tuning Reduce Instruction Sensitivity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26661)
*Jaekeol Choi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文研究监督微调（SFT）对大语言模型指令敏感性（即同一任务不同指令改写下的性能变异）的影响。通过Qwen3不同规模模型及跨模型家族实验发现：SFT并非均匀降低指令敏感性，其鲁棒性效果取决于适配设置；且评估协议（自由生成 vs 强制选择）会导致定性不同的鲁棒性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在同一任务的不同指令改写下表现差异大，但传统任务特定SFT如何改变这种指令敏感性尚不清楚。论文旨在量化SFT对指令敏感性的影响，并揭示模型规模、训练指令选择及评估方式对敏感性测量的影响。

Method: 定义指令敏感性为多条指令改写下任务性能的标准差。使用Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B在MS MARCO上进行受控规模分析，并用Mistral-7B和Gemma-2-9B做跨家族验证。采用查询级自助法进行配对对比统计检验。在ESCI-English上对比自由生成与基于似然的强制选择评估协议。

Result: 1) SFT前，指令随Qwen3规模增大急剧下降。2) 1.7B和4B模型经SFT后敏感性一致降低54%-71%。3) 8B模型个体敏感性变化不显著，但训练指令间的配对对比在三个随机种子下方向一致且统计可靠。4) Gemma-2-9B呈现与Qwen3-8B相同的方向性对比，Mistral-7B则不然。5) 不同评估协议在生成有效标签率近乎完美、平均性能相似时，仍得出定性不同的鲁棒性结论。

Conclusion: SFT不均匀降低指令敏感性：其鲁棒性效应依赖于适配设置（模型规模、训练指令选择）；且测量到的敏感性还取决于预测与评分协议（自由生成 vs 强制选择）。这提示在评估模型鲁棒性时需同时考虑适配设置与评估方式。

Abstract: Large language models can exhibit substantial performance variation across alternative formulations of the same task instruction, yet it remains unclear how conventional task-specific supervised fine-tuning (SFT) changes this instruction sensitivity. We study this question by evaluating fixed model checkpoints under multiple paraphrased instructions and defining instruction sensitivity as the standard deviation of task performance across them. We conduct a controlled scale analysis with Qwen3 models at 1.7B, 4B, and 8B on MS MARCO, together with targeted cross-family checks using Mistral-7B and Gemma-2-9B. Before SFT, instruction sensitivity decreases sharply with Qwen3 model scale. At 1.7B and 4B, SFT consistently reduces sensitivity across training instructions, with reductions of approximately 54--71%. At 8B, individual sensitivity changes are not statistically distinguishable from zero, but paired contrasts between training instructions are statistically reliable under query-level bootstrap analysis and have consistent directions across all three random seeds. Gemma-2-9B shows the same directional training-instruction contrast as Qwen3-8B, whereas Mistral-7B does not, suggesting that the strength of this effect also varies across models. Experiments on ESCI-English further show that free-generation and likelihood-based forced-choice evaluation can yield qualitatively different robustness conclusions even when valid-label generation is nearly perfect and average task performance is similar. Overall, SFT does not uniformly reduce instruction sensitivity: its robustness effect depends on the adaptation setting, while measured sensitivity can additionally depend on the prediction and scoring protocol.

</details>


### [296] [BLANC: Discovering Patent White Space via Changes in Normalized Pointwise Mutual Information Between Multi-View Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26685)
*Shuichi Miyazawa,Kensuke Fujii*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: BLANC是一种三阶段流水线，用于识别专利领域中的空白区域，结合多视角神经主题建模、NPMI量化跨维度关联、条件检测ΔNPMI指标，实验显示能有效回收被人为耗尽的技术组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖人工专利映射或单视角聚类，缺乏定量空白检测，需要一种能自动识别“全局已建立、局部未探索”技术组合的方法。

Method: 三阶段流水线：(1)沿应用/用途、新颖性、创造性步骤三个语义维度的多视角神经主题建模；(2)使用归一化点互信息(NPMI)量化跨维度簇关联；(3)条件检测，当语料按用户指定关键词过滤时NPMI下降的组合被标记，引入ΔNPMI指标捕捉“全局已建立、局部未探索”的组合。

Result: 在USPTO ML/AI(5417专利)和玻璃成分(1982专利)语料上，当目标对75%文档被移除时，BLANC分别回收34.1%和27.3%的耗尽组合，而随机移除或不同组合移除的诱饵试验中目标从未被回收(191次)。单视角合并恢复率为零，先前共现度量在随机移除下也会标记目标，缺乏特异性。专有案例中，关键词“氟”揭示了氟表面处理×抑制翘曲候选(ΔNPMI高达0.48)，专家独立确认。

Conclusion: BLANC通过多视角建模和ΔNPMI指标有效识别专利空白区域，优于基线方法，在真实专有案例中也被专家验证，为战略研发规划提供定量工具。

Abstract: Identifying white space --- the unexplored but potentially valuable regions of a patent landscape --- is essential for strategic R&D planning, yet existing methods rely on manual patent mapping or apply single-view clustering without quantitative gap detection. We propose BLANC (Blank Landscape Analysis through NPMI Conditioning), a three-phase pipeline combining (1) multi-view neural topic modeling along three semantic dimensions (application/use, novelty, inventive step); (2) Normalized Pointwise Mutual Information (NPMI) to quantify cross-dimensional cluster association; and (3) conditional detection that flags combinations whose NPMI drops when the corpus is filtered by a user-specified keyword. The drop is captured by a new metric, $Δ$NPMI, which identifies combinations "established globally, unexplored locally." Because white space has no ground truth, we evaluate BLANC on two public USPTO corpora --- machine learning/AI (5,417 patents, CPC G06N) and glass compositions (1,982 patents, CPC C03C) --- by artificially depleting known technology combinations and testing recovery. When three-quarters of a target pair's documents are removed, BLANC recovers 34.1% (ML/AI) and 27.3% (glass) of the depleted combinations, whereas size-matched removals not aimed at them (random documents, or those of a different established combination) essentially never do: the target is never recovered in 191 decoy trials. Collapsing the three semantic views into one recovers nothing, while prior co-occurrence measures also flag the target under random removal, offering no specificity. In a proprietary case (302 float glass / glass-ceramics patents), the keyword "fluorine" reveals a fluorine surface treatment $\times$ warpage suppression candidate ($Δ$NPMI up to 0.48) that experts had independently identified.

</details>


### [297] [Beyond a Single Story: Meta-Reviewing Sparse and Incomplete User-generated Contents for Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26728)
*Hongren Wang,Tianjun Wei,Yingpeng Du,Jie Zhang,Yin-Leng Theng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 MOSAIC 模型，通过元评论机制聚合邻居用户的属性-情感证据，结合多门控混合专家架构和注意力模块，解决用户生成内容（UGC）的稀疏性和不完整性问题，同时提升推荐精度与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户生成内容（如文本评论）虽能捕捉细粒度偏好，但面临两大挑战：(1) 缺失评论——交互无对应评论；(2) 不完整评论——现有评论仅覆盖部分相关属性。现有方法忽视这些 UGC 特有问题，导致推荐精度下降。

Method: MOSAIC 为每个目标用户构建元评论：从邻居用户评论中聚合属性-情感证据；采用多门控混合专家（MMoE）架构联合优化评分预测与元评论属性-情感预测；引入注意力模块将聚合的元评论信号个性化适配至目标用户，输出精细化评分预测与属性级解释。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上实验表明，MOSAIC 在推荐准确性和解释质量上持续超越最先进基线，有效缓解 UGC 稀疏性与不完整性，且对交互历史有限的用户也能带来稳定增益。

Conclusion: MOSAIC 通过元评论机制与 MMoE 联合建模，成功解决 UGC 稀疏与不完整问题，实现了高精度推荐与细粒度可解释性的双重提升，为数据稀疏场景下的推荐系统提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Data sparsity remains a long-standing challenge in recommender systems, and it becomes more severe for methods relying on user-generated content (UGC) such as textual reviews, which capture fine-grained preferences but require more user efforts to produce. As a result, UGC exhibits (1) missing reviews, where interactions lack any review, and (2) incomplete reviews, where available reviews cover only a subset of relevant attributes. Existing approaches often overlook these UGC-specific issues, leading to degraded accuracy. Motivated by meta-review in academic peer review, we propose MOSAIC (Meta-review On Sparse And Incomplete user-generated Content), which constructs a meta-review for each target user by aggregating attribute-sentiment evidence from neighbor users' reviews. A multi-gate mixture-of-experts (MMoE) architecture jointly optimizes rating prediction and meta-review attribute-sentiment prediction, while an attention module personalizes the aggregated meta-review signals to each target user, yielding both refined rating predictions and attribute-level explanations. Experiments on four real-world datasets demonstrate that MOSAIC consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in both recommendation accuracy and explanation quality, mitigating UGC sparsity and incompleteness while delivering consistent gains for users with limited interaction history.

</details>


### [298] [STREAM: An Objective-Driven and Uncertainty-Aware Framework for Industrial Energy Data Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26754)
*Zhipeng Ma,Bo Nørregaard Jørgensen,Zheng Grace Ma*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出STREAM框架，一种目标驱动且不确定性感知的工业能源管理数据获取框架，通过两个工业案例验证，证明数据可访问性不等于分析适用性，并展示STREAM如何支持透明决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业能源管理需要将能源使用与设备状态、生产批次、物料流和工艺条件相关联的数据集，但传统获取工作流常只重连接和存储，未验证可访问信号是否满足能源绩效评估需求。

Method: STREAM框架包含六个阶段：目标规范、技术需求、资源映射、源提取、归档元数据、迁移数据库。增加了阶段级工件、最小证据门控、源适用性规则、元数据模板、不确定性评分表和案例特定追溯矩阵。通过铸造厂感应炉熔炼和奶酪粉干燥两个工业批量工艺案例验证，使用SCADA和生产订单数据。

Result: 结果表明数据可访问性不等于分析适用性，STREAM支持对即时数据使用、分析限制和优先基础设施改进的透明决策。

Conclusion: STREAM提供端到端的目标到数据可追溯性输出，在所有六个阶段评估测量、时间、上下文和处理不确定性，为工业能源管理数据获取提供了系统化、不确定性感知的方法论。

Abstract: Industrial energy management requires datasets that connect energy use with equipment states, production batches, material flows, and process conditions. However, conventional acquisition workflows commonly emphasize connectivity and storage without verifying whether accessible signals satisfy the requirements of a defined energy-performance assessment. This paper presents STREAM, an objective-driven and uncertainty-aware framework comprising Specification of Objectives, Technical Requirements, Resource Mapping, Extraction from Sources, Archival Metadata, and Migration to Database. STREAM is the central workflow: objective-to-data traceability is its end-to-end output, while measurement, temporal, contextual, and processing uncertainty are assessed across all six stages. Compared with the original conceptual STREAM sequence, this paper adds stage-level artifacts, minimum-evidence gates, source-suitability rules, a metadata template, an uncertainty rubric, and case-specific traceability matrices. The framework is validated through two industrial batch-process cases: induction-furnace melting in a foundry and cheese-powder drying using SCADA and production-order data. The results demonstrate that data accessibility is not equivalent to analytical suitability and show how STREAM supports transparent decisions about immediate data use, analytical restrictions, and prioritized infrastructure improvements.

</details>


### [299] [When Memory Takes Gradients: Collaborative Vector Memory for Agentic Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26895)
*Hanchong Chen,Xing Tang,Lingjie Li,Xiongfeng Shan,Xiuqiang He*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 CoVeMem（协作向量记忆），用冻结的 LightGCN 用户/物品向量替代基于文本的智能体记忆，通过对比对齐与列表级联合训练让 LLM 直接读取向量记忆并进行推荐排序，在四个基准上以零额外 LLM 记忆维护调用达到或超越最强文本记忆基线 19/20 指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体推荐系统用文本叙事作为持久化用户记忆，存在两大缺陷：1) 文本记忆需逐次重写，利用完整交互历史代价高昂；2) 协作证据（全目录上的梯度相似度）难以翻译为自然语言句子保留下来。

Method: 1) 记忆库：冻结 LightGCN 得到的用户/物品嵌入作为向量记忆库。2) 检索：每次决策时，候选集自身从记忆库检索最相关历史状态，作为软令牌连同轻量文本画像送入 LLM 上下文。3) 训练：先用物品语义锚点做对比对齐，再用掩码候选做列表级联合训练，教会模型读取向量状态并通过它们排序；最后用逐点是/否读出头对每个候选打分。

Result: 在四个指令驱动推荐基准上，CoVeMem 在 20 个指标单元中 19 个匹配或超越最强协作文本记忆智能体，且记忆维护零额外 LLM 调用（文本记忆需每交互一次）。

Conclusion: 向量记忆使梯度可回传至完整交互历史，让‘记什么’与‘怎么读’均可端到端训练，突破了文本记忆的扩展与表达瓶颈，为智能体推荐提供了可扩展、可微分的记忆范式。

Abstract: Agentic recommender systems ground each decision of a large language model (LLM) in a persistent memory of the user, and in existing agents that memory is text: a narrative written and maintained by further LLM calls. Text limits this memory in two ways. It is updated one rewrite at a time, so exploiting the full interaction history is prohibitively expensive; and collaborative evidence, graded similarity over an entire catalog, does not survive translation into sentences. We propose CoVeMem (Collaborative Vector Memory), which vectorizes the collaborative core of the agent's memory. Frozen LightGCN user and item states form the memory bank; at each decision, the candidate set itself retrieves the most relevant historical states, which enter the LLM's context as soft tokens alongside a light textual profile. Contrastive alignment to item-semantic anchors, followed by listwise co-training with masked candidates, teaches the model to read these states and to rank through them; a pointwise yes/no readout scores each candidate. Across four instruction-grounded recommendation benchmarks, CoVeMem matches or exceeds the strongest collaborative text-memory agent on 19 of 20 metric cells while requiring zero additional LLM calls for memory maintenance beyond the shared static profile, against per-interaction calls for text memory. The memory now takes gradients: the full interaction history, out of reach for text, becomes available as training data for what the agent remembers and for how it reads what it remembers.

</details>


### [300] [Topology-Masked Unified Backbone for Joint Feature Interaction and Multi-Domain Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27005)
*Zhihao Zhu,Dezheng Han,Jikang Xia,Shuaishuai Guo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MaskRec提出了一种拓扑掩码统一token交互架构，将异构特征、多域行为序列和上下文信号统一为token表示，引入全局记忆token和域级记忆token，设计结构化注意力掩码TopoMask，在同一拓扑约束注意力过程中实现异构特征交互和多域序列建模，并加入双路交互式查询生成模块注入候选条件用户-物品交互信号。在腾讯广告算法大赛数据集上实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模点击后转化率预测需要联合建模异构特征交互和多域用户行为序列依赖。现有工业排序模型通常用独立模块处理这两方面，近期统一架构虽尝试融合但依赖模块间协调，未能将所有信息源完全组织在同一交互空间。

Method: MaskRec将异构特征、多域行为序列、上下文信号转换为统一token表示，引入可学习全局记忆token和域级记忆token作为信息聚合节点；设计结构化注意力掩码TopoMask，根据不同信息源的结构差异和建模需求选择性启用或阻断注意力连接；在统一token空间内通过拓扑约束注意力过程同时进行异构特征交互和多域序列建模；还引入双路交互式查询生成模块，在统一骨干网络前注入候选条件的用户-物品交互信号。

Result: 在腾讯广告算法大赛数据集上实验表明，MaskRec相比官方基线实现了稳定提升。

Conclusion: MaskRec提出的统一框架对于工业CVR预测有效，通过拓扑掩码统一token交互架构解决了异构特征交互与多域序列建模的联合建模问题。

Abstract: Large-scale post-click conversion rate (CVR) prediction requires jointly modeling heterogeneous feature interactions and dependencies over multi-domain user behavior sequences. Existing industrial ranking models usually handle these two aspects with separate modules. Recent unified architectures attempt to incorporate them into a single framework, but such unification often relies on coordination between modules and does not fully organize all information sources within the same interaction space. To address this problem, we propose MaskRec, a topology-masked unified token interaction architecture for feature interaction and multi-domain sequence modeling. MaskRec transforms heterogeneous features, multi-domain behavior sequences, and contextual signals into unified token representations, and further introduces learnable global memory tokens and domain-level memory tokens as information aggregation nodes. Based on this unified token space, MaskRec designs a structured attention mask, TopoMask, which selectively enables or blocks attention connections according to the structural differences and modeling requirements of different information sources. In this way, heterogeneous feature interaction and multi-domain sequence modeling are performed within the same topology-constrained attention process. In addition, MaskRec incorporates a dual-path interactive query generation module to inject candidate-conditioned user--item interaction signals before the unified backbone. Experiments on the Tencent Advertising Algorithm Competition dataset show that MaskRec achieves stable improvements over the official baseline, validating the effectiveness of the proposed unified framework for industrial CVR prediction.

</details>


### [301] [Conversational Recommendation over Live E-Commerce Catalogues with Self-Refreshing Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27006)
*Ante Kapetanovic,Tomislav Duricic,Dionizije Fa,Andro Mercep,Emanuel Lacic*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种面向实时变动电商目录的、与商家无关的多轮对话式购物助手，核心是自刷新检索器，通过增量同步实现向量索引的低延迟更新，对话层采用控制器架构，仅用LLM做意图分类和偏好引导，其余检索、重排、多样性选择由专用函数完成，已在WhatsApp上演示，目录变更可在下一次同步后生效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的对话推荐系统多在静态、预建索引的商品集上评测，但真实电商目录会持续变动（增删、改价、补货），导致静态索引无法反映最新库存与价格，亟需一种能实时同步目录变更的对话式推荐架构。

Method: 1) 自刷新检索器：摄入商家商品Feed，丰富记录后写入向量索引；逐项哈希识别新增、变更、删除、未变四类，仅处理增量而非全量重建。2) 控制器式对话层：LLM仅负责意图分类与偏好引导；检索、重排、多样性选择作为独立确定性函数运行，保证可控与低延迟。3) 端到端演示：接入WhatsApp，目录变更经下一次成功同步后即在推荐中生效。

Result: 系统实现了商家无关的多轮对话购物助手，支持实时目录变更的增量同步，避免了全量重建开销；对话层解耦LLM与检索逻辑，提升了可控性与响应速度；已发布可运行的WhatsApp聊天机器人、文档与演示视频。

Conclusion: 通过自刷新检索器与控制器式对话层的组合，该工作给出了一套可落地的、面向实时电商目录的对话推荐范式，证明了增量索引同步与功能解耦架构在生产环境中的可行性。

Abstract: Conversational recommender systems based on large language models (LLMs) are usually evaluated on static, pre-indexed item collections, yet e-commerce catalogues change continuously as products are added or removed, repriced, and restocked. We present a merchant-agnostic, multi-turn conversational shopping assistant that operates over such live catalogues. Its central component is a self-refreshing retriever that ingests a merchant product feed, enriches the records, and synchronizes them into a vector index. On each run, per-item hashes identify which products are new, changed, deleted, or unchanged, so only the delta is processed rather than rebuilding the whole catalogue. A controller-based dialogue layer consumes this index, using an LLM only for intent classification and preference elicitation while retrieval, reranking, and diversity selection run as dedicated functions. Our demonstration is a WhatsApp shopping assistant in which catalogue changes reach the recommendations after the next successful sync. A live chatbot, documentation, and a recorded walkthrough are available at https://github.com/infobip/infobip-agentic-crs.

</details>


### [302] [ProRetrieval: Learning to Orchestrate Hybrid Search via Executable Program Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27017)
*Chengsong You,Zhen Sun,Yunhai Hu,Junwei Zhou,Xiaoyu Cao,Binyu Li,Ziyan Zhao,Weiyao Wang,Liren Lu,Zhijie Ye,Yumo Cao,Yitao Long,Yiwei Xu,Qiyi Jiang,Xuanyi Fu,Yufan Chen,Yilun Li,Rongkang Xiong,Yiran Zou,Nan Du*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ProRetrieval提出了一种将语言模型作为检索编排器的新范式，通过混合DSL（SQL+向量检索原语）合成可执行程序，实现结构化约束与语义意图的任意布尔逻辑组合，并在电商和邮件基准上以4B模型超越GPT-5.5和Claude Opus 4.7。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实检索需将结构化约束与语义意图通过任意布尔逻辑组合，现有混合管线（如RRF、self-querying）仅支持固定组合形式，RL检索器仅将LM作为单一后端的查询生成器，无法编排异构检索路径。

Method: 将LM作为检索编排器，合成混合DSL程序（交织SQL算子与文本/图像向量检索原语），利用SQL提供的逻辑代数融合异构候选集；采用GRPO和DAPO在分层四项奖励下训练Qwen3-4B。

Result: 在Amazon商品和Enron邮件两个新基准上，4B模型Hit@1分别达0.81和0.91，显著优于GPT-5.5（0.69/0.86）、Claude Opus 4.7及全套检索、LLM增强、结构化查询、图基线。

Conclusion: ProRetrieval通过程序化编排实现了灵活、可组合的异构检索，小模型经强化学习可超越大型闭源模型，为复杂现实检索提供了可扩展的新范式。

Abstract: Real-world retrieval often composes structured constraints with semantic intents over text and images through arbitrary Boolean logic. Existing hybrid pipelines such as reciprocal rank fusion or self-querying retrievers admit only a fixed form of composition, while recent reinforcement-learning retrievers train the language model as a query generator for a single backend, leaving the orchestration of heterogeneous retrieval paths outside its action space. We propose ProRetrieval, which recasts the language model as a retrieval orchestrator: given a natural-language query, it synthesizes an executable program in a hybrid DSL interleaving SQL operators over structured fields with vector-retrieval primitives over text and images, with SQL itself providing the logical algebra that fuses heterogeneous candidate sets. We train Qwen3-4B with GRPO and DAPO under a hierarchical four-term reward, and evaluate on two new benchmarks built from Amazon products and Enron email. Our 4B model surpasses GPT-5.5 (Hit@1 0.81 vs. 0.69 on e-commerce; 0.91 vs. 0.86 on email) and Claude Opus 4.7 and a comprehensive suite of retrieval, LLM-augmented, structured-query, and graph-based baselines. Code: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ProRetrieval/; data: https://huggingface.co/datasets/anonymous-7219/ProRetrieval.

</details>


### [303] [Astar: Learning to Propose Evolution Directions for Self-Evolving Industrial AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27287)
*Jinxin Hu,Hao Deng,Haibo Xing,Lingyu Mu,Muyu Zou,Weiqin Yang,Sirui Chen,Bohao Wang,Zhezheng Hao,Hao Zhang,Zulong Chen,Shizhun Wang,Yu Zhang,Xiaoyi Zeng,Jiawei Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Astar，一种基于训练的专用进化引导模型，从工业系统的迭代历史中学习，自动化提出有效的演化方向。Astar-8B在阿里巴巴Lazada广告系统部署，单次提案成功率0.6786，远超人类专家(0.3229)和通用LLM(0.3071)，实现了20轮连续全自动迭代，离线Hitrate@200提升23.6%，在线A/B测试GMV提升4.86%，广告收入提升1.82%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI系统通过持续迭代推进，但提出有效演化方向的起点仍严重依赖资深专家，通用LLM（甚至GPT-5.5）只能给出泛泛且错位的建议，因为所需专业知识源于经验积累而非显性编码，难以直接注入。

Method: Astar通过两大方面解决四大挑战（稀疏监督、噪声数据、巨大方向空间、昂贵验证）：数据侧设计管道将噪声历史提交转化为大规模干净进化语料，采用成对样本扩展和噪声过滤；模型侧采用中间训练、SFT、RL三阶段训练，用层级提示引导演化方向生成，RL中的奖励模型作为快速替代评估器。

Result: Astar-8B在Lazada广告系统实测：单次提案成功率0.6786，显著高于人类专家0.3229和最强通用LLM 0.3071；指导20轮连续迭代（两周），离线Hitrate@200提升23.6%，在线A/B测试GMV相对提升4.86%，广告收入相对提升1.82%。

Conclusion: Astar证明了通过训练专用模型可实现AI系统演化方向的自动化提出，闭环了迭代流程，显著超越人类专家和通用LLM，在工业级推荐系统中取得实质性业务指标提升，为全自动AI系统进化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Modern AI systems advance through continuous iteration: a loop of proposing evolution directions, implementing code, training, and evaluation. While the latter three stages are increasingly automated, the starting point --- proposing effective evolution directions --- remains a critical bottleneck that still relies heavily on senior experts. In this work, we explore whether AI can take over this role. We find that general-purpose LLMs, even the advanced GPT-5.5, offer only generic and misaligned suggestions: the required expertise is accumulated through experience rather than explicitly codified, and thus hard to inject directly.
  To this end, we propose Astar, a training-based approach that learns a specialized evolution-guiding model from the abundant iteration histories of industrial systems. Realizing this idea, however, raises four challenges: sparse supervision, noisy data, a vast direction space, and prohibitively expensive verification. We address them along two fronts. On the data side, we design a pipeline that turns noisy historical commits into a large, clean evolutionary corpus via pairwise sample expansion and noise filtering. On the model side, we train the model through mid-training, SFT, and RL, guiding evolution direction generation with hierarchical hints and using the reward model in RL as a fast surrogate evaluator.
  Astar has been deployed in Alibaba's Lazada advertising system for evolution direction proposal. Astar-8B achieves a single-proposal success rate of 0.6786 in real-execution evaluation, far exceeding human experts (0.3229) and the strongest general-purpose LLM (0.3071). More importantly, Astar closes the loop and enables fully automatic iteration: it guided 20 consecutive iterations over two weeks, improving offline Hitrate@200 by 23.6%, while an online A/B test yielded relative lifts of 4.86% in GMV and 1.82% in advertising revenue.

</details>


### [304] [Stageboost: Recommending Signals Based on Counterfactual Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27366)
*Darpan Singhal,Matan Mandelbrod,Tal Franji,Manasa Kolla,Vipul Gaba,Yuri Brovman*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: eBay提出了一种基于XGBoost的两阶段模型，用于优化View-Item页面上的信号展示，实验显示整体GMB提升0.08%，零配件类目GMB提升0.58%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: eBay的View-Item页面展示信号（短文本/视觉片段）以提供商品上下文信息，旨在促进智能购买决策并激励用户互动，需要一种最优策略来决定展示哪些信号。

Method: 采用两阶段XGBoost模型：第一阶段可能用于候选信号筛选或排序，第二阶段用于最终展示决策优化，通过机器学习方法最优化填充VI页面信号。

Result: 在线实验显示：整体GMB（成交总额）提升0.08%，零配件类目GMB提升0.58%，主要归因于高客单价商品转化率的提升。

Conclusion: 基于XGBoost的两阶段建模方法能有效优化eBay商品页面信号展示策略，显著提升业务指标，特别是在高价值商品和特定类目上效果更为明显。

Abstract: Signals are short textual or visual snippets displayed on the eBay View-Item (VI) page, providing additional, contextual information for users about the viewed item. The aim of displaying these signals is to facilitate intelligent purchase and to incentivize engagement. In this paper, we present a 2 stage xgboost based model that optimally populates the VI page with signals. This approach has shown a 0.08% lift in overall GMB (Gross Merchandise Bought) and 0.58% increase in Parts and Accessories GMB, primarily due to increase in conversion of high average price items in online experimentation.

</details>


### [305] [Scaling Graph Neural Networks for Friend Recommendation: Multi-Hash User Embeddings and Temporal Neighbor Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27413)
*Maksim Utushkin,Andrei Ovsiannikov,Alexander D'yakonov*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个面向生产级社交图的可扩展端到端GNN排序系统，核心创新包括多哈希ID嵌入（将嵌入表大小减少98%以上）和基于时间戳排序的CSR存储配合二分搜索的时间邻居采样（将采样复杂度从O(deg(v)+k)降至O(log(deg(v))+k)）。在1.94亿用户、280亿边的图上验证，线上A/B测试显示好友添加量提升16%，独立添加者提升11.5%，并开源分布式训练推理框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业界大规模社交图（亿级用户、千亿级边）部署消息传递GNN面临建模与系统双重挑战：高基数节点ID的嵌入表超过200GB，且现有时间邻居采样需扫描全邻接表，对高度节点不可行。需要一种既保持排序质量又大幅降低存储和计算开销的解决方案。

Method: 1. 多哈希ID嵌入：将多哈希作为节点主要表示，替代全嵌入表，压缩率>98%。 2. 时间邻居采样优化：采用按时间戳排序的CSR存储结构，利用二分搜索定位时间窗口，将单节点采样复杂度从线性降为对数级。 3. 端到端分布式训练推理框架：支持大规模时序图上的GNN排序。

Result: 离线消融实验证实两项设计各自贡献；线上A/B测试相对强基线：好友推荐添加量+16%，独立添加用户数+11.5%。系统在194M用户、28B边图上成功部署并开源。

Conclusion: 多哈希嵌入与高效时间采样的组合解决了生产级社交图GNN部署的核心存储与计算瓶颈，显著提升推荐效果，并提供了可复用的分布式框架，为大规模时序图学习提供了实用范式。}

Abstract: Friend recommendation is inherently graph-structured: the relevance of a potential connection depends on multi-hop social context rather than user attributes alone. However, deploying message-passing GNNs on a production-scale social graph with hundreds of millions of users and tens of billions of edges requires addressing numerous modeling and systems challenges. We present a scalable end-to-end GNN ranking system for production social graphs, focusing on two design choices that are critical in this setting: multi-hash ID embeddings and temporal neighbor sampling. Multi-hash embeddings are common for high-cardinality features, but industrial GNN systems typically either ignore trainable IDs or accept full embedding tables, exceeding 200 GB for our graph. We integrate multi-hash as the primary node representation, reducing the ID-embedding table size by more than 98 percent while preserving ranking quality. Temporal neighbor sampling is well understood in principle, but existing implementations scan full adjacency lists, which is a non-starter for users with tens of thousands of friends. We implement timestamp-sorted CSR storage with binary search, reducing the per-node temporal sampling cost from $O(deg(v) + k)$ to $O(\log(deg(v)) + k)$. Beyond these components, we show that this combination scales and yields measurable production impact. On a graph with 194M users and 28B edges, offline ablations isolate each design choice's contribution. In an online A/B test, our system increases friend additions from recommendations by 16 percent and unique friend adders by 11.5 percent over a strong production baseline. We release our framework for distributed training and inference on large temporal graphs.

</details>


### [306] [misi: a Metric Inverted Sample Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27422)
*Edgar Chavez*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 misi，一种基于随机采样词汇表的倒排索引，用于通用度量空间的近似最近邻搜索。索引构建可并行化、确定性、内存占用低，适合频繁重建语料库、批量相似度工作负载和受限内存服务场景，但在查询速度上不如饱和图基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有近似最近邻搜索方法在构建成本、确定性、内存占用和黑盒度量支持方面存在权衡。需要一种索引，能以线性大小的词汇表泛化 NAPP 索引，保持倒排列表长度恒定，并将任意高召回率索引组合为更大规模的索引，同时提供理论召回保证。

Method: misi 使用大小与 n 成正比的随机样本作为词汇表。每个对象由其 k_b 个最近样本点表示，通过可插拔内层索引在样本上查找。查询通过 IDF 加权共享邻居投票后，对 C 个候选进行精确验证。构建过程为 n 次独立搜索，极易并行化。概率模型给出召回保证：k_b 在重叠间隙上对数级增长即可，验证预算由索引自身估计。

Result: 构建 10^8 向量在 64 核上耗时 5,250 秒，比同召回率图构建快 3.7 倍；流式构建受限于 3 GiB 内存上限；服务 10^8 向量从 NVMe 读取，内存预算 8 GB，低于 SSD 图基线的工作底线。查询速度方面，饱和图基线在内存中快 6-16 倍；0.99 召回率的验证预算随 n^0.30 增长。所有结果附带种子、饱和扫描、完整配置，并包含测得的负面结果。

Conclusion: misi 的优势在于结构性：构建并行、确定性、内存占用小、支持黑盒度量。适用于构建成本、确定性、内存占用或黑盒度量比峰值吞吐更重要的场景：频繁重建语料库、批量相似度工作负载、受限内存服务。代价是查询时计算量较大。

Abstract: We present misi, an inverted index for approximate nearest-neighbor search over general metric spaces whose vocabulary is a random sample of the database, of size proportional to $n$. Each object is represented by its $k_b$ nearest sample points, found by a pluggable inner index over the sample; queries are answered by an idf-weighted shared-neighbor vote followed by exact verification of $C$ candidates. The construction generalizes the NAPP index from a constant number of pivots to a linear-size vocabulary, which keeps posting lists at constant expected length $ρ= k_b/α$ as $n$ grows and turns the index into a combinator: any high-recall index on $αn$ points yields an index on $n$ points, for any metric. A probabilistic model gives a recall guarantee -- $k_b$ logarithmic in $n$ over the overlap gap suffices, with a verification budget the index itself estimates -- and a matching limit: the vote cannot resolve overlap differences below order $1/\sqrt{k_b}$. The design's strengths are structural: construction is $n$ independent searches -- embarrassingly parallel, deterministic, $5{,}250$ s for $10^8$ vectors on 64 cores, $3.7\times$ faster than a matched-recall graph build -- it streams under an enforced 3 GiB cap, and the portable artifact serves $10^8$ vectors from NVMe within an enforced 8 GB budget, below the working floor of the SSD-graph baseline. Its cost is query-time work: saturated graph baselines answer $6$-$16\times$ faster in RAM, and the verification budget for 0.99 recall grows as $n^{0.30}$. All results carry seeds, saturation sweeps and full configurations, are generated from run manifests, and include measured negative results. The intended applications weight construction cost, determinism, memory footprint, or black-box metrics over peak throughput: frequently rebuilt corpora, batch similarity workloads, constrained-memory serving.

</details>
